\u003ccode\u003eKHR_materials_unlit\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eOverview\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-overview\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Overview\" href=\"#overview\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe core glTF 2.0 material model includes an \u003ccode\u003eemissiveFactor\u003c/code\u003e and an \u003ccode\u003eemissiveTexture\u003c/code\u003e to control the color and\nintensity of the light being emitted by the material, clamped to the range [0.0, 1.0]. However, in PBR environments\nwith high-dynamic range reflections and lighting, stronger emission effects may be desirable.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIn this extension, a new \u003ccode\u003eemissiveStrength\u003c/code\u003e scalar factor is supplied, that governs the upper limit of emissive\nstrength per material.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eImplementation Note\u003c/strong\u003e: This strength can be colored and tempered using the core material's \u003ccode\u003eemissiveFactor\u003c/code\u003e\nand \u003ccode\u003eemissiveTexture\u003c/code\u003e controls, permitting the strength to vary across the surface of the material.\nSupplying values above 1.0 for \u003ccode\u003eemissiveStrength\u003c/code\u003e can have an influence on\nreflections, tonemapping, blooming, and more.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePhysical Units\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-physical-units\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Physical Units\" href=\"#physical-units\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis extension supplies a unitless multiplier to the glTF 2.0 specification's emissive factor and\ntexture.  Including this multiplier does not alter the physical units defined in glTF 2.0's\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.khronos.org/registry/glTF/specs/2.0/glTF-2.0.html#additional-textures\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eadditional textures section\u003c/a\u003e,\nunder the \u003cstrong\u003eemissive\u003c/strong\u003e texture.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eExtending Materials\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-extending-materials\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Extending Materials\" href=\"#extending-materials\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cem\u003eThis section is non-normative.\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAny material with an \u003ccode\u003eemissiveFactor\u003c/code\u003e (and optionally an \u003ccode\u003eemissiveTexture\u003c/code\u003e) can have its strength modulated\nor amplified by the inclusion of this extension.  For example:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"{\n    \u0026quot;materials\u0026quot;: [\n        {\n            \u0026quot;emissiveFactor\u0026quot;: [\n                1.0,\n                1.0,\n                1.0\n            ],\n            \u0026quot;emissiveTexture\u0026quot;: {\n                \u0026quot;index\u0026quot;: 3\n            },\n            \u0026quot;extensions\u0026quot;: {\n                \u0026quot;KHR_materials_emissive_strength\u0026quot;: {\n                    \u0026quot;emissiveStrength\u0026quot;: 5.0\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    ]\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e{\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"materials\"\u003c/span\u003e: [\n        {\n            \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"emissiveFactor\"\u003c/span\u003e: [\n                \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1.0\u003c/span\u003e,\n                \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1.0\u003c/span\u003e,\n                \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1.0\u003c/span\u003e\n            ],\n            \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"emissiveTexture\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n                \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"index\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e3\u003c/span\u003e\n            },\n            \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"extensions\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n                \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"KHR_materials_emissive_strength\"\u003c/span\u003e: {\n                    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\"emissiveStrength\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e5.0\u003c/span\u003e\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    ]\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIn the above example, the \u003ccode\u003eemissiveFactor\u003c/code\u003e has been set to its maximum value, to enable the \u003ccode\u003eemissiveTexture\u003c/code\u003e.\nThe \u003ccode\u003eemissiveStrength\u003c/code\u003e has been set to 5.0, making the texture five times brighter than it otherwise\nwould have been.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eParameters\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-parameters\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Parameters\" href=\"#parameters\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe following parameters are contributed by the \u003ccode\u003eKHR_materials_emissive_strength\u003c/code\u003e extension:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eName\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eType\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eDescription\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eRequired\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eemissiveStrength\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003enumber\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eThe maximum strength of the emissive texture.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNo, default: \u003ccode\u003e1.0\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" KHR\\_materials\\_emissive\\_strength","","## Contributors","","* Mike Bond, Adobe, [@miibond](https://github.com/MiiBond)","* Alex Jamerson, Amazon","* Thomas Dideriksen, Amazon","* Alex Wood, Analytical Graphics, [@abwood](https://twitter.com/abwood)","* Ed Mackey, Analytical Graphics, [@emackey](https://twitter.com/emackey)","* Nicolas Savva, Autodesk [@nicolassavva-autodesk](https://github.com/nicolassavva-autodesk)","* Henrik Edstrom, Autodesk","* Tobias Haeussler, Dassault Systemes [@proog128](https://github.com/proog128)","* Bastian Sdorra, [@lexaknyazev](https://github.com/lexaknyazev)","* Bruce Cherniak, Intel","* Gary Hsu, Microsoft [@bghgary](https://twitter.com/bghgary)","* Nicholas Barlow, Microsoft","* Adam Morris, Target [@weegeekps](https://github.com/weegeekps)","* Sandra Voelker, Target","* Ben Houston, Threekit, [@bhouston](https://twitter.com/BenHouston3D)","* Eric Chadwick, Wayfair [echadwick-wayfair](https://github.com/echadwick-wayfair)","","Copyright (C) 2021 The Khronos Group Inc. All Rights Reserved. glTF is a trademark of The Khronos Group Inc.","See [Appendix](#appendix-full-khronos-copyright-statement) for full Khronos Copyright Statement.","","## Status","","Draft","","## Dependencies","","Written against the glTF 2.0 spec.","","## Exclusions","","* This extension must not be used on a material that also uses `KHR_materials_unlit`.","","## Overview","","The core glTF 2.0 material model includes an `emissiveFactor` and an `emissiveTexture` to control the color and","intensity of the light being emitted by the material, clamped to the range [0.0, 1.0]. However, in PBR environments","with high-dynamic range reflections and lighting, stronger emission effects may be desirable.","","In this extension, a new `emissiveStrength` scalar factor is supplied, that governs the upper limit of emissive","strength per material.","","**Implementation Note**: This strength can be colored and tempered using the core material's `emissiveFactor`","and `emissiveTexture` controls, permitting the strength to vary across the surface of the material.","Supplying values above 1.0 for `emissiveStrength` can have an influence on","reflections, tonemapping, blooming, and more.","","### Physical Units","","This extension supplies a unitless multiplier to the glTF 2.0 specification's emissive factor and","texture.  Including this multiplier does not alter the physical units defined in glTF 2.0's","[additional textures section](https://www.khronos.org/registry/glTF/specs/2.0/glTF-2.0.html#additional-textures),","under the **emissive** texture.","","## Extending Materials","","*This section is non-normative.*","","Any material with an `emissiveFactor` (and optionally an `emissiveTexture`) can have its strength modulated","or amplified by the inclusion of this extension.  For example:","","```json","{","    \"materials\": [","        {","            \"emissiveFactor\": [","                1.0,","                1.0,","                1.0","            ],","            \"emissiveTexture\": {","                \"index\": 3","            },","            \"extensions\": {","                \"KHR_materials_emissive_strength\": {","                    \"emissiveStrength\": 5.0","                }","            }","        }","    ]","}","```","","In the above example, the `emissiveFactor` has been set to its maximum value, to enable the `emissiveTexture`.","The `emissiveStrength` has been set to 5.0, making the texture five times brighter than it otherwise","would have been.","","### Parameters","","The following parameters are contributed by the `KHR_materials_emissive_strength` extension:","","| Name                   | Type       | Description                                    | Required           |","|------------------------|------------|------------------------------------------------|--------------------|","| **emissiveStrength**   | `number`   | The maximum strength of the emissive texture.  | No, default: `1.0` |","","","## Implementation Notes","","*This section is non-normative.*","","A typical (pseudocode) implementation might look like the following:","","```","color += emissiveFactor.rgb * sRGB_to_Linear(emissiveTexture.rgb) * emissiveStrength;","```","","## Schema","","- [glTF.KHR_materials_emissive_strength.schema.json](schema/glTF.KHR_materials_emissive_strength.schema.json)","","## Appendix: Full Khronos Copyright Statement","","Copyright 2021 The Khronos Group Inc.","","Some parts of this Specification are purely informative and do not define requirements","necessary for compliance and so are outside the Scope of this Specification. These","parts of the Specification are marked as being non-normative, or identified as","**Implementation Notes**.","","Where this Specification includes normative references to external documents, only the","specifically identified sections and functionality of those external documents are in","Scope. However, in PBR environments","with high-dynamic range reflections and lighting, stronger emission effects may be desirable.","","In this extension, a new \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eemissiveStrength\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e scalar factor is supplied, that governs the upper limit of emissive","strength per material.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eImplementation Note\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e: This strength can be colored and tempered using the core material\u0026#39;s \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eemissiveFactor\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e","and \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eemissiveTexture\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e controls, permitting the strength to vary across the surface of the material.","Supplying values above 1.0 for \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eemissiveStrength\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e can have an influence on","reflections, tonemapping, blooming, and more.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e### \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003ePhysical Units\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","This extension supplies a unitless multiplier to the glTF 2.0 specification\u0026#39;s emissive factor and","texture.  Including this multiplier does not alter the physical units defined in glTF 2.0\u0026#39;s","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e[\u003c/span\u003eadditional textures section\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e]\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-corl\"\u003ehttps://www.khronos.org/registry/glTF/specs/2.0/glTF-2.0.html#additional-textures\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e,","under the \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eemissive\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e texture.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e## \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eExtending Materials\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e*\u003c/span\u003eThis section is non-normative.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e*\u003c/span\u003e","","Any material with an \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eemissiveFactor\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e (and optionally an \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eemissiveTexture\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e) can have its strength modulated","or amplified by the inclusion of this extension.  For example:","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e```\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003ejson\u003c/span\u003e","{","    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026quot;materials\u0026quot;\u003c/span\u003e: [","        {","            \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026quot;emissiveFactor\u0026quot;\u003c/span\u003e: [","                \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1.0\u003c/span\u003e,","                \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1.0\u003c/span\u003e,","                \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1.0\u003c/span\u003e","            ],","            \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026quot;emissiveTexture\u0026quot;\u003c/span\u003e: {","                \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026quot;extensions\u0026quot;\u003c/span\u003e: {","                \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026quot;KHR_materials_emissive_strength\u0026quot;\u003c/span\u003e: {","                    \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026quot;emissiveStrength\u0026quot;\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e5.0\u003c/span\u003e","                }","            }","        }","    ]","}","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e```\u003c/span\u003e","","In the above example, the \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eemissiveFactor\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e has been set to its maximum value, to enable the \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eemissiveTexture\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e.","The \u003cspan <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Status</h2><a id="user-content-status" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Status" href="#status"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Draft</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Dependencies</h2><a id="user-content-dependencies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Dependencies" href="#dependencies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Written against the glTF 2.0 spec.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Exclusions</h2><a id="user-content-exclusions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Exclusions" href="#exclusions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>This extension must not be used on a material that also uses <code>KHR_materials_unlit</code>.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Overview</h2><a id="user-content-overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Overview" href="#overview"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The core glTF 2.0 material model includes an <code>emissiveFactor</code> and an <code>emissiveTexture</code> to control the color and
intensity of the light being emitted by the material, clamped to the range [0.0, 1.0]. However, in PBR environments
with high-dynamic range reflections and lighting, stronger emission effects may be desirable.</p>
<p dir="auto">In this extension, a new <code>emissiveStrength</code> scalar factor is supplied, that governs the upper limit of emissive
strength per material.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Implementation Note</strong>: This strength can be colored and tempered using the core material's <code>emissiveFactor</code>
and <code>emissiveTexture</code> controls, permitting the strength to vary across the surface of the material.
Supplying values above 1.0 for <code>emissiveStrength</code> can have an influence on
reflections, tonemapping, blooming, and more.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Physical Units</h3><a id="user-content-physical-units" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Physical Units" href="#physical-units"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This extension supplies a unitless multiplier to the glTF 2.0 specification's emissive factor and
texture.  Including this multiplier does not alter the physical units defined in glTF 2.0's
<a href="https://www.khronos.org/registry/glTF/specs/2.0/glTF-2.0.html#additional-textures" rel="nofollow">additional textures section</a>,
under the <strong>emissive</strong> texture.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Extending Materials</h2><a id="user-content-extending-materials" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Extending Materials" href="#extending-materials"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><em>This section is non-normative.</em></p>
<p dir="auto">Any material with an <code>emissiveFactor</code> (and optionally an <code>emissiveTexture</code>) can have its strength modulated
or amplified by the inclusion of this extension.  For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
    &quot;materials&quot;: [
        {
            &quot;emissiveFactor&quot;: [
                1.0,
                1.0,
                1.0
            ],
            &quot;emissiveTexture&quot;: {
                &quot;index&quot;: 3
            },
            &quot;extensions&quot;: {
                &quot;KHR_materials_emissive_strength&quot;: {
                    &quot;emissiveStrength&quot;: 5.0
                }
            }
        }
    ]
}"><pre>{
    <span class="pl-ent">"materials"</span>: [
        {
            <span class="pl-ent">"emissiveFactor"</span>: [
                <span class="pl-c1">1.0</span>,
                <span class="pl-c1">1.0</span>,
                <span class="pl-c1">1.0</span>
            ],
            <span class="pl-ent">"emissiveTexture"</span>: {
                <span class="pl-ent">"index"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">3</span>
            },
            <span class="pl-ent">"extensions"</span>: {
                <span class="pl-ent">"KHR_materials_emissive_strength"</span>: {
                    <span class="pl-ent">"emissiveStrength"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">5.0</span>
                }
            }
        }
    ]
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">In the above example, the <code>emissiveFactor</code> has been set to its maximum value, to enable the <code>emissiveTexture</code>.
The <code>emissiveStrength</code> has been set to 5.0, making the texture five times brighter than it otherwise
would have been.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Parameters</h3><a id="user-content-parameters" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Parameters" href="#parameters"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The following parameters are contributed by the <code>KHR_materials_emissive_strength</code> extension:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Name</th>
<th>Type</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Required</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>emissiveStrength</strong></td>
<td><code>number</code></td>
<td>The maximum strength of the emissive texture.</td>
<td>No, default: <code>1.0</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Implementation Notes</h2><a id="user-content-implementation-notes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Implementation Notes" href="#implementation-notes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><em>This section is non-normative.</em></p>
<p dir="auto">A typical (pseudocode) implementation might look like the following:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="color += emissiveFactor.rgb * sRGB_to_Linear(emissiveTexture.rgb) * emissiveStrength;"><pre class="notranslate"><code>color += emissiveFactor.rgb * sRGB_to_Linear(emissiveTexture.rgb) * emissiveStrength;
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Schema</h2><a id="user-content-schema" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Schema" href="#schema"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

</ul>
